correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n   294→    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n   295→        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n   296→        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n   297→        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n   298→\n   299→    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n   300→        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n   301→        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n   302→            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n   303→            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n   304→                return view, traj\n   305→        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n   306→\n   307→    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n   308→    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n   309→        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n   310→        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n   311→        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n   312→        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n   313→        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   314→        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n   315→\n   316→    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n   317→    def _velocity(self, track):\n   318→        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n   319→        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n   320→        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n   321→        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n   322→            return time, speed\n   323→\n   324→        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n   325→        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n   326→\n   327→        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n   328→        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n   329→            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n   330→                continue\n   331→            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   332→            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n   333→            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n   334→                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n   335→        return time, speed\n   336→\n   337→    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n   338→        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n   339→        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n   340→        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n   341→\n   342→    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n   343→    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n   344→    def tongue_velocity(self):\n   345→        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   346→        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   347→        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   348→        side = side / self.side_scale\n   349→        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n   350→        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n   351→\n   352→    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n   353→    def paw_velocity(self):\n   354→        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   355→        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n   356→\n   357→    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n   358→    def motion_energy(self):\n   359→        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   360→        rows = []\n   361→        for trial in self.trials:\n   362→            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n   363→            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n   364→            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n   365→        return np.array(rows)\n   366→\n   367→    def outputs(self):\n   368→        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n   369→        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n   370→        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n   371→        energy = self.motion_energy()\n   372→        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   373→                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n   374→                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n   375→\n   376→\n   377→# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n   378→# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n   379→# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n   380→# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n   381→\n   382→def _quality(cluster):\n   383→    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n   384→    label = cluster['quality']\n   385→    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n   386→\n   387→\n   388→class Neural:\n   389→    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n   390→\n   391→    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n   392→        self.obj = obj\n   393→        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n   394→        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n   395→                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n   396→                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n   397→\n   398→        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n   399→        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n   400→                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n   401→        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n   402→\n   403→    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n   404→        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n   405→        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n   406→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n   407→            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n   408→            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n   409→                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n   410→        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n   411→\n   412→    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n   413→        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n   414→        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n   415→        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n   416→\n   417→        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n   418→        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n   419→        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n   420→        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n   421→\n   422→    def rates(self):\n   423→        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n   424→        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n   425→        rates = []\n   426→        for cluster in self.clusters:\n   427→            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n   457→\n   458→    return {\n   459→        'session_id': name,\n   460→        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n   461→        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n   462→        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   463→        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n   464→        'n_trials': n_trials,\n   465→        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n   466→    }\n   467→\n   468→\n   469→def assemble(results):\n   470→    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n   471→    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n   472→    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n   473→\n   474→    return {\n   475→        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   476→        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   477→        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   478→\n   479→        'subjects': subjects,\n   480→        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n   481→\n   482→        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n   483→        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n   484→\n   485→        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   486→        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   487→        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   488→\n   489→        'metadata': {\n   490→            'task_description':\n   491→                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. In the delayed-response '\n   492→                'context a tone instructs the direction, a delay follows, and an auditory '\n   493→                'go cue opens the response window; in the water-cued context all cues are '\n   494→                'omitted and water arrives at a random port. struct\n        return {k: _h5(f, node[k]) for k in node}\n    if node.attrs.get('MATLAB_class', b'').decode() == 'char':\n        return ''.join(chr(c) for c in np.asarray(node[()]).ravel())\n    if node.dtype == object:                                # Early-lick and photostim trials\n# are dropped, following the paper. The index keeps the original trial numbers, which\n# every later step uses to reach back into the raw arrays.\n\ndef trial_column(bp, *names):\n    \"\"\"One entry per trial from a field of bp, e.g. trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable').\"\"\"\n    n_trials = int(np.ravel(bp['Ntrials'])[0])\n    field = bp\n    for name in names:\n        field = field[name]\n    return np.ravel(np.asarray(field, float))[:n_trials]\n\n\ndef trial_info(bp):\n    \"\"\"Outcome, context, and lick direction per trial, without early-lick or photostim trials.\"\"\"\n    hit = trial_column(bp, 'hit') > 0\n    miss = trial_column(bp, 'miss') > 0\n    right = trial_column(bp, 'R') > 0\n    autowater = trial_column(bp, 'autowater') > 0\n    early = trial_column(bp, 'early') > 0\n    stim = trial_column(bp, 'stim', 'enable') > 0\n    n_trials = hit.size\n\n    # a hit is a correct lick and a miss an incorrect one; the animal ignored the rest\n    outcome = np.full(n_trials, OUTCOME['ignore'])\n    outcome[hit] = OUTCOME['correct']\n    outcome[miss] = OUTCOME['incorrect']\n\n    # water is given from a random port without any cue in the WC context\n    context = np.where(autowater, CONTEXT['WC'], CONTEXT['DR'])\n\n    # a hit licks the instructed port, a miss licks the other one, an ignore neither\n    lick_direction = np.full(n_trials, LICK['no lick'])\n    lick_direction[hit] = np.where(right[hit], LICK['right'], LICK['left'])\n    lick_direction[miss] = np.where(right[miss], LICK['left'], LICK['right'])\n\n    info = pd.DataFrame({'lick_direction': lick_direction, 'context': context,\n                         'outcome': outcome})\n    info.index.name = 'trial'\n    return info[~early & ~stim]\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- camera --\n# Tongue velocity, paw velocity, and motion energy for one session. A class because the\n# video offset, the per-view scales, and the median split are session-wide\n# quantities that every trial reuses.\n\ndef _feature(traj, index):\n    \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature, whichever way the file orders ts.\"\"\"\n    ts = np.asarray(traj['ts'])\n    if ts.shape[0] == len(traj['featNames']):      # v7.3 reads it as (features, xyl, frames)\n        return ts[index]\n    return ts[:, :, index].T                       # v5 keeps MATLAB's (frames, xyl, features)\n\n\ndef _runs(valid):\n    \"\"\"First and last+1 index of each contiguous run of valid frames.\"\"\"\n    edges = np.diff(np.concatenate([[0], valid.astype(int), [0]]))\n    return zip(np.flatnonzero(edges == 1), np.flatnonzero(edges == -1))\n\n\ndef _bin_frames(time, values):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the valid frames in each bin, NaN where a bin holds no valid frame.\"\"\"\n    bin_of_frame = np.searchsorted(BIN_EDGES, time, side='right') - 1\n    valid = np.isfinite(values) & (bin_of_frame >= 0) & (bin_of_frame < N_BINS)\n    total = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], weights=values[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    count = np.bincount(bin_of_frame[valid], minlength=N_BINS)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _mean_over_available(first, second):\n    \"\"\"Mean of the two, whichever one is there alone, or NaN where neither is.\"\"\"\n    stacked = np.stack([first, second])\n    count = np.sum(np.isfinite(stacked), axis=0)\n    total = np.nansum(stacked, axis=0)\n    return np.where(count > 0, total / np.maximum(count, 1), np.nan)\n\n\ndef _discretize(values, threshold):\n    \"\"\"Two classes split at the threshold; untracked bins take the trailing class.\"\"\"\n    code = np.where(values >= threshold, MOVEMENT['above threshold'], MOVEMENT['below threshold'])\n    return np.where(np.isnan(values), MOVEMENT['not visible'], code)\n\n\nclass Camera:\n    \"\"\"The three camera streams of a session, binned and cut into classes.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, name, trials, datadir):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.trials = list(trials)\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.offset = self._video_offset()             # one constant for the whole session\n        self.energy = load_motion_energy(name, datadir)\n\n        # frame resolution velocity, for the two tongue views and for the paw\n        self.side_tongue = self._trial_velocity(SIDE_TONGUE)\n        self.bottom_tongue = self._trial_velocity(BOTTOM_TONGUE)\n        self.paw = self._trial_velocity(PAW)\n\n        # the two cameras see the tongue at different pixel scales\n        self.side_scale = self._view_scale(self.side_tongue)\n        self.bottom_scale = self._view_scale(self.bottom_tongue)\n\n    def _trial_velocity(self, feature):\n        \"\"\"Frame resolution velocity of one tracked feature, one entry per trial.\"\"\"\n        return {trial: self._velocity(self._track(trial, feature)) for trial in self.trials}\n\n    def _video_offset(self):\n        \"\"\"Seconds by which the video clock leads the behavior clock (findVideoOffset.m).\"\"\"\n        sample_rate = float(np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['fs'])[0])\n        video_start = np.ravel(self.obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']) / sample_rate\n        behavior_start = trial_column(self.obj['bp'], 'ev', 'bitStart')\n        return pd.Series(video_start).mode().iloc[0] - pd.Series(behavior_start).mode().iloc[0]\n\n    def _traj(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Tracking of one trial and camera, however the file stores it.\"\"\"\n        traj = self.obj['traj'][view]\n        if isinstance(traj, dict):                     # v7.3: one entry per camera\n            return {field: traj[field][trial] for field in traj}\n        entry = traj[trial]                            # v5: one entry per trial\n        if hasattr(entry, '_fieldnames'):              # scipy leaves nested structs as objects\n            return {field: getattr(entry, field) for field in entry._fieldnames}\n        return entry\n\n    # camera frame time after correcting for the video offset and aligning to the go cue, in seconds\n    def _frame_time(self, trial, view):\n        \"\"\"Frame times of one trial and camera, in seconds from go cue onset.\"\"\"\n        frame_times = np.ravel(self._traj(trial, view)['frameTimes'])\n        return frame_times - self.offset - self.go_cue[trial]\n\n    def _camera_of(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"The camera that tracks a feature, and its tracking for this trial.\"\"\"\n        for view in (SIDE, BOTTOM):\n            traj = self._traj(trial, view)\n            if feature in list(traj['featNames']):     # the name picks out the camera\n                return view, traj\n        raise KeyError('%s is tracked by neither camera' % feature)\n\n    # x, y, and likelihood within the time window\n    def _track(self, trial, feature):\n        \"\"\"x, y, and likelihood of one feature over the frames inside the window.\"\"\"\n        view, traj = self._camera_of(trial, feature)\n        x, y, likelihood = _feature(traj, list(traj['featNames']).index(feature))\n        time = self._frame_time(trial, view)           # frame counts can differ between views\n        inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n        return time[inside], x[inside], y[inside], likelihood[inside]\n\n    # smooth the x, y postion, and compute the speed, filter by the likelihood threshold > 0.90\n    def _velocity(self, track):\n        \"\"\"Combined velocity over the valid frames, NaN elsewhere.\"\"\"\n        time, x, y, likelihood = track\n        speed = np.full(time.size, np.nan)\n        if time.size < 2:                              # a few trials have no frame times\n            return time, speed\n\n        sigma = SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS / 1000 / np.median(np.diff(time))\n        valid = likelihood > LIKELIHOOD_CUT            # x and y are NaN below this\n\n        # smooth and differentiate inside each run of tracked frames, never across a gap\n        for start, stop in _runs(valid):\n            if stop - start < 2:                       # a lone frame has no velocity\n                continue\n            sx = gaussian_filter1d(x[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            sy = gaussian_filter1d(y[start:stop], sigma, mode='mirror')\n            speed[start:stop] = np.hypot(np.gradient(sx, time[start:stop]),\n                                         np.gradient(sy, time[start:stop]))\n        return time, speed\n\n    def _view_scale(self, velocity):\n        \"\"\"The percentile that puts one camera's velocity on a comparable scale.\"\"\"\n        pooled = np.concatenate([velocity[trial][1] for trial in self.trials])\n        return np.nanpercentile(pooled, NORMALIZE_PCT)\n\n    # the tongue is seen by both cameras, on different pixel scales, so each view is\n    # binned and divided by its own percentile before the two are averaged\n    def tongue_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned tongue velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        side = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.side_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        bottom = np.array([_bin_frames(*self.bottom_tongue[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n        side = side / self.side_scale\n        bottom = bottom / self.bottom_scale\n        return _mean_over_available(side, bottom)      # one view alone where the other is missing\n\n    # only one paw stays tracked through the delay, so it is used on its own, in pixels\n    def paw_velocity(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned paw velocity, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        return np.array([_bin_frames(*self.paw[trial]) for trial in self.trials])\n\n    # motion energy is already one number per frame, so it only needs binning\n    def motion_energy(self):\n        \"\"\"Binned motion energy, as (n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        rows = []\n        for trial in self.trials:\n            time = self._frame_time(trial, SIDE)       # motion energy follows the side camera\n            inside = (time >= T_START) & (time <= T_STOP)\n            rows.append(_bin_frames(time[inside], self.energy[trial][inside]))\n        return np.array(rows)\n\n    def outputs(self):\n        \"\"\"The three streams, each cut in two at its own session median.\"\"\"\n        tongue = self.tongue_velocity()\n        paw = self.paw_velocity()\n        energy = self.motion_energy()\n        return (_discretize(tongue, np.nanpercentile(tongue, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(paw, np.nanpercentile(paw, SPLIT_PCT)),\n                _discretize(energy, np.nanpercentile(energy, SPLIT_PCT)))\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------- neural --\n# Spike times are already on the behavior clock and relative to trial start, so aligning\n# to the go cue is one subtraction. Quality is a free text label written with either case\n# and with a few typos, so it is matched in lower case.\n\ndef _quality(cluster):\n    \"\"\"Quality label of a cluster; a handful carry no label at all.\"\"\"\n    label = cluster['quality']\n    return label.strip().lower() if isinstance(label, str) else ''\n\n\nclass Neural:\n    \"\"\"The QC-passing units of a session, as smoothed firing rates.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, obj, probes, trials):\n        self.obj = obj\n        self.go_cue = trial_column(obj['bp'], 'ev', 'goCue')\n        self.clusters = [cluster for probe in probes                # probes concatenated\n                         for cluster in self._probe_clusters(probe)\n                         if _quality(cluster) not in QUALITY_DROP]\n\n        # two sessions keep running after the recording stops, leaving trials with no spikes\n        last = max(int(np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int).max(initial=0))\n                   for cluster in self.clusters)\n        self.trials = np.asarray([t for t in trials if t < last])   # trial numbers are 1 based\n\n    def _probe_clusters(self, probe):\n        \"\"\"One dict per cluster on the given probe, however the file stores them.\"\"\"\n        entry = self.obj['clu'][probe - 1]\n        if isinstance(entry, dict) and isinstance(entry['tm'], list):\n            # v7.3: one entry per probe, each field holding one array per cluster\n            return [{field: entry[field][i] for field in entry}\n                    for i in range(len(entry['tm']))]\n        return self.obj['clu']                     # v5: one entry per cluster, single probe\n\n    def spike_count(self, cluster):\n        \"\"\"Spikes of one cluster per trial and bin, aligned to the go cue.\"\"\"\n        spike_trial = np.asarray(cluster['trial'], int) - 1         # trial numbers are 1 based\n        spike_time = np.asarray(cluster['trialtm'], float) - self.go_cue[spike_trial]\n\n        # count spikes into a trial by bin grid; those outside the window fall off the edges\n        trial_edges = np.arange(self.go_cue.size + 1) - 0.5\n        counts, _, _ = np.histogram2d(spike_trial, spike_time, bins=[trial_edges, BIN_EDGES])\n        return counts[self.trials]                                  # only the trials we keep\n\n    def rates(self):\n        \"\"\"Firing rate of every unit above MIN_RATE, as (n_units, n_trials, N_BINS).\"\"\"\n        sigma = RATE_SD_MS / 1000 / BIN\n        rates = []\n        for cluster in self.clusters:\n            rate = self.spike_count(cluster) / BIN                 # counts -> Hz\n            if rate.mean() > MIN_RATE:\n                rates.append(gaussian_filter1d(rate, sigma, axis=1, mode='reflect'))\n        return np.asarray(rates, np.float32)\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ session --\n\ndef process_session(name, datadir):\n    \"\"\"Convert one session into per-trial neural, input, and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    obj = load_mat(session_file(name, 'data_structure', datadir))\n    info = trial_info(obj['bp'])\n\n    neural = Neural(obj, SESSIONS[name], info.index)\n    info = info.loc[neural.trials]                 # drops any trial past the recording\n    n_trials = len(info)\n\n    rates = neural.rates()\n    tongue, paw, energy = Camera(obj, name, info.index, datadir).outputs()\n\n    # the three per-trial values are repeated across bins so all six outputs share one array\n    out = np.empty((n_trials, len(OUTPUT_NAMES), N_BINS), np.int8)\n    out[:, 0, :] = info['lick_direction'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 1, :] = info['context'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 2, :] = info['outcome'].to_numpy()[:, None]\n    out[:, 3, :] = tongue\n    out[:, 4, :] = paw\n    out[:, 5, :] = energy\n\n    inp = np.tile(TIME.astype(np.float32), (n_trials, 1, 1))       # (n_trials, 1, N_BINS)\n\n    return {\n        'session_id': name,\n        'subject': name.split('_')[0],\n        'neural': [np.ascontiguousarray(rates[:, t, :]) for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'input': [inp[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'output': [out[t] for t in range(n_trials)],\n        'n_trials': n_trials,\n        'n_units': int(rates.shape[0]),\n    }\n\n\ndef assemble(results):\n    \"\"\"Stitch per-session results into the target dictionary.\"\"\"\n    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results})\n    sub_ix = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    return {\n        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([sub_ix[r['subject']] for r in results], np.int32),\n\n        'brain_regions': [BRAIN_REGION],\n        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_units'], np.int32) for r in results],\n\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n\n        'metadata': {\n            'task_description':\n                'Head-fixed mice lick left or right for water. In the delayed-response '\n                'context a tone instructs the direction, a delay follows, and an auditory '\n                'go cue opens the response window; in the water-cued context all cues are '\n                'omitted and water arrives at a random port. Decoded variables: lick '\n                'direction, behavioral context, outcome, and tongue, paw, and whole-body '\n                'movement.',\n            'time_bin_size': BIN * 1000.0,          # ms\n            'temporal_alignment_event': 'go cue onset (auditory chirp, 10 ms)',\n            'off_start': T_START,\n            'off_end': T_STOP,\n\n            'dataset': 'Hasnain, Birnbaum et al., Nat Neurosci 2024, ALM recordings',\n            'neural_units': 'Hz',\n            'session_selection':\n                \"The 44 sessions listed in the author's DataLoadingScripts/Recording and \"\n                'video/load<ANM>_ALMVideo.m that are present in data/, with the probe each '\n                'entry specifies; two-probe sessions are concatenated.',\n            'unit_curation':\n                'Cluster quality labels, lower cased, excluding %s, then units whose mean '\n                'rate over the window is at or below %.1f Hz.'\n                % (', '.join(sorted(QUALITY_DROP)), MIN_RATE),\n            'trial_curation': 'Early-lick and photostimulation trials excluded.',\n            'neural_processing':\n                'Spikes counted in %.0f ms bins, converted to Hz, smoothed with a gaussian '\n                'of %d ms standard deviation.' % (BIN * 1000, RATE_SD_MS),\n            'movement_discretisation':\n                'Tongue and paw velocity are the speed of the DeepLabCut position after a '\n                '%d ms gaussian, computed within each run of frames whose likelihood '\n                'exceeds %.1f. The two tongue views are each divided by their %dth '\n                'percentile before being averaged. Each stream is averaged into %.0f ms '\n                'bins and split at its own session %dth percentile; bins the camera did '\n                'not track take the trailing \"not visible\" class.'\n                % (SMOOTH_SIGMA_MS, LIKELIHOOD_CUT, NORMALIZE_PCT, BIN * 1000, SPLIT_PCT),\n            'session_info': [\n                {'session_id': r['session_id'], 'subject': r['subject'],\n                 'n_trials': r['n_trials'], 'n_units': r['n_units']}\n                for r in results\n            ],\n        },\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', help='output pickle path')\n    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='process all sessions (default)')\n    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='process only 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--datadir', default=DATA_DIR,\n                    help='directory holding the Ephys_Behavior/ folders (default: %(default)s)')\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    names = SAMPLE if args.sample else list(SESSIONS)\n    print('%d sessions' % len(names), flush=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    results = []\n    for i, name in enumerate(names, 1):\n        t1 = time.time()\n        res = process_session(name, args.datadir)\n        results.append(res)\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        print('[%2d/%d] %-18s %4d trials %4d units  %5.1fs  (elapsed %5.1fs, total ~%5.1fs)'\n              % (i, len(names), name, res['n_trials'], res['n_units'],\n                 time.time() - t1, elapsed, elapsed / i * len(names)), flush=True)\n\n    data = assemble(results)\n    print('\\nconverted in %.1f s' % (time.time() - t0), flush=True)\n    print('sessions %d | subjects %d | trials %d | units %d'\n          % (len(results), len(data['subjects']),\n             sum(r['n_trials'] for r in results), sum(r['n_units'] for r in results)),\n          flush=True)\n\n    with open(args.outfile, 'wb') as f:\n        pickle.dump(data, f, protocol=pickle.HIGHEST_PROTOCOL)\n    print('wrote %s (%.1f GB)'\n          % (args.outfile, os.path.getsize(args.outfile) / 1e9), flush=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":571,"startLine":1,"totalLines":571}}}

('JEB24', '2023-10-31', [1], 'RandomizedDelay_Ephys_Behavior'),\n    81→    ('JEB24', '2023-11-02', [1], 'RandomizedDelay_Ephys_Behavior'),\n    82→    ('JEB24', '2023-11-03', [1], 91→ALIGN_EVENT = 'goCue'\n    92→TMIN = -2.5  # seconds\n    93→TMAX = 2.5   # seconds\n    94→DT = 1.0 / 100  # 10 ms bins\n    95→SMOOTH_WINDOW = 15  # bins, causal Gaussian\n    96→BC_TYPE = 'reflect'\n    97→LOW_FR = 1.0  # Hz, minimum firing rate\n    98→EXCLUDED_QUALITIES = {'garbage', 'gabrga', 'noisy', 'real?'}\n    99→VIDEO_FR = 400  # Hz\n   100→DATA_ROOT = '/app/data'\n   101→\n   102→# ============================================================\n   103→# SMOOTHING (matching mySmooth.m)\n   104→# ============================================================\n   105→\n   106→def causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n   107→    \"\"\"Causal Gaussian smoothing matching mySmooth.m.\n   108→    x: (T,) or (T, C) array\n   109→    N: window size in bins\n   110→    \"\"\"\n   111→    if N <= 1:\n   112→        return x\n   113→\n   114→    was_1d = x.ndim == 1\n   115→    if was_1d:\n   116→        x = x[:, None]\n   117→\n   118→    # Build causal Gaussian kernel\n   119→    kern = np.array(scipy_windows.gaussian(N, std=N/6.0))  # gausswin equivalent\n   120→    kern[:N//2] = 0  # causal: zero out first half\n   121→    kern = kern / kern.sum()\n   122→\n   123→    # Handle boundary conditions\n   124→    if bctype == 'reflect':\n   125→        x_filt = np.concatenate([x[:N], x], axis=0)\n   126→        trim = N\n   127→    elif bctype == 'zeropad':\n   128→        x_filt = np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, x.shape[1])), x], axis=0)\n   129→        trim = N\n   130→    else:  # 'none'\n   131→        x_filt = x\n   132→        trim = 0\n   133→\n   134→    out = np.zeros_like(x_filt)\n   135→    for j in range(x_filt.shape[1]):\n   136→        out[:, j] = np.convolve(x_filt[:, j], kern, mode='same')\n   137→    out = out[trim:]\n   138→\n   139→    if was_1d:\n   140→        out = out[:, 0]\n   141→    return out\n   142→\n   143→\n   144→# ============================================================\n   145→# DATA LOADING\n   146→# ============================================================\n   147→\n   148→def _load_v5_session(data_path):\n   149→    \"\"\"Load a MATLAB v5 format session file and convert to mat73-like dict structure.\"\"\"\n   150→    import scipy.io\n   151→    raw = scipy.io.loadmat(data_path, squeeze_me=False)\n   152→    o = raw['obj'][0, 0]\n   153→\n   154→    obj = {}\n   155→\n   156→    # bp (behavioral data)\n   157→    bp_raw = o['bp'][0, 0]\n   158→    bp = {}\n   159→    for field in bp_raw.dtype.names:\n   160→        val = bp_raw[field]\n   161→        if field == 'ev':\n   162→            ev_raw = val[0, 0]\n   163→            ev = {}\n   164→            for ef in ev_raw.dtype.names:\n   165→                ev_val = ev_raw[ef]\n   166→                if ev_val.dtype == object:\n   167→                    # Cell array (e.g., lickL, lickR)\n   168→                    ev[ef] = [ev_val.flat[i] for i in range(ev_val.size)]\n   169→                else:\n   170→                    ev[ef] = ev_val.flatten()\n   171→            bp['ev'] = ev\n   172→        elif field == 'stim':\n   173→            stim_raw = val[0, 0] if val.size > 0 and val.dtype.names else None\n   174→            if stim_raw is not None and stim_raw.dtype.names:\n   175→                stim = {}\n   176→                for sf in stim_raw.dtype.names:\n   177→                    sv = stim_raw[sf]\n   178→                    if sv.dtype == object:\n   179→                        stim[sf] = sv\n   180→                    else:\n   181→                        stim[sf] = sv.flatten()\n   182→                bp['stim'] = stim\n   183→            else:\n   184→                bp['stim'] = {'enable': np.zeros(int(bp_raw['Ntrials'].flat[0]))}\n   185→        elif val.dtype == object:\n   186→            bp[field] = val\n   187→        else:\n   188→            try:\n   189→                if val.size == 1:\n   190→                    bp[field] = float(val.flat[0])\n   191→                else:\n   192→                    bp[field] = val.flatten()\n   193→            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n   194→                bp[field] = val\n   195→    obj['bp'] = bp\n   196→\n   197→    # clu (cluster data)\n   198→    clu_raw = o['clu']\n   199→    n_probes = clu_raw.shape[1] if clu_raw.ndim >= 2 else 1\n   200→    clu_list = []\n   201→    for p in range(n_probes):\n   202→        probe_raw = clu_raw[0, p] if clu_raw.ndim >= 2 else clu_raw[0]\n   203→        if probe_raw is None or probe_raw.size == 0:\n   204→            clu_list.append(None)\n   205→            continue\n   206→        n_clusters = probe_raw.shape[1] if probe_raw.ndim >= 2 else probe_raw.shape[0]\n   207→        probe = {}\n   208→        for field in probe_raw.dtype.names:\n   209→            vals = []\n   210→            for c in range(n_clusters):\n   211→                v = probe_raw[0, c][field] if probe_raw.ndim >= 2 else probe_raw[c][field]\n   212→                if field == 'quality':\n   213→                    vals.append(str(v.flat[0]).strip() if v.size > 0 else '')\n   214→                elif field in ('tm', 'trialtm', 'trial'):\n   215→                    vals.append(v.flatten())\n   216→                elif field == 'site' or field == 'channel':\n   217→                    vals.append(v.flatten() if v.size > 0 else np.array([]))\n   218→                else:\n   219→                    vals.append(v)\n   220→            probe[field] = vals\n   221→        clu_list.append(probe)\n   222→    obj['clu'] = clu_list\n   223→\n   224→    # sglx\n   225→    sglx_raw = o['sglx'][0, 0]\n   226→    sglx = {}\n   227→    for field in sglx_raw.dtype.names:\n   228→        val = sglx_raw[field]\n   229→        if field == 'bitcode':\n   230→            bc_raw = val[0, 0]\n   231→            bc = {}\n   232→            for bf in bc_raw.dtype.names:\n   233→                bv = bc_raw[bf]\n   234→                if bv.dtype == object:\n   235→                    bc[bf] = bv\n   236→                else:\n   237→                    bc[bf] = bv.flatten()\n   238→            sglx['bitcode'] = bc\n traj\n   246→    traj_raw = o['traj']\n   247→    n_views = traj_raw.shape[1] if traj_raw.ndim >= 2 else traj_raw.shape[0]\n   248→    traj_list = []\n   249→    for v in range(n_views):\n   250→        view_raw = traj_raw[0, v] if traj_raw.ndim >= 2 else traj_raw[v]\n   251→        if view_raw is None or view_raw.size == 0:\n   252→            traj_list.append(None)\n   253→            continue\n   254→        n_trials_traj = view_raw.shape[1] if view_raw.ndim >= 2 else view_raw.shape[0]\n   255→        view = {}\n   256→        for field in view_raw.dtype.names:\n   257→            vals = []\n   258→            for t in range(n_trials_traj):\n   259→                trial_data = view_raw[0, t] if view_raw.ndim >= 2 else view_raw[t]\n   260→                v_data = trial_data[field]\n   261→                if field == 'featNames':\n   262→                    if v_data.dtype == object:\n   263→                        names = []\n   264→                        for fi in range(v_data.size):\n               else:\n   272→                        vals.append(v_data)\n   273→                elif field == 'frameTimes':\n   274→                    vals.append(v_data.flatten())\n   275→                elif field == 'ts':\n   276→                    vals.append(v_data)\n   277→                elif field == 'fn':\n   278→                    vals.append(str(v_data.flat[0]) if v_data.size > 0 else 284→\n   285→    # ex\n   286→    ex_raw = o['ex'][0, 0]\n   287→    ex = {}\n   288→    for field in ex_raw.dtype.names:\n   289→        val = ex_raw[field]\n   290→        if val.dtype.kind in ('U', obj\n   297→\n   298→\n   299→def load_session_data(anm, date, data_dir):\n   300→    \"\"\"Load a session's data structure and motion energy.\"\"\"\n   301→    import mat73\n   302→    import scipy.io\n   303→\n   304→    data_path = os.path.join(DATA_ROOT, data_dir, f'data_structure_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   305→    t0 = time.time()\n   306→    try:\n   307→        obj = mat73.loadmat(data_path)['obj']\n   308→    except TypeError:\n   309→        # MATLAB v5 format - use scipy and convert to similar structure\n   310→        obj = _load_v5_session(data_path)\n   311→    t_load = time.time() - t0\n   312→\n   313→    # Load motion energy\n   314→    me_path = os.path.join(DATA_ROOT, data_dir, f'motionEnergy_{anm}_{date}.mat')\n   315→    me_raw = None\n   316→    me_thresh = None\n   317→    if os.path.exists(me_path):\n   318→        me_file = scipy.io.loadmat(me_path)\n   319→        me_var = me_file['me']\n   320→\n   321→        if me_var.dtype.names:\n   322→            # Structured array (me is a struct with .data and .moveThresh)\n   323→            me_struct = me_var[0, 0]\n   324→            me_data = me_struct['data']\n   325→            # Handle nested struct: if me.data is itself a struct with .data field\n   326→            if me_data.dtype.names and 'data' in me_data.dtype.names:\n   327→                inner = me_data[0, 0]\n   328→                me_raw = inner['data']\n   329→                me_thresh = float(inner['moveThresh'].flat[0])\n   330→            else:\n   331→                me_raw = me_data\n   332→                me_thresh = float(me_struct['moveThresh'].flat[0])\n   333→        elif me_var.dtype == object:\n   334→            # Direct cell array of motion energy per trial (no struct wrapper)\n   335→            me_raw = me_var\n   336→            me_thresh = None  # No threshold available\n   337→\n   338→    return obj, me_raw, me_thresh, t_load\n   339→\n   340→\n   341→def compute_video_offset(obj):\n   342→    \"\"\"Compute video-neural offset matching findVideoOffset.m.\"\"\"\n   343→    bitStart = scipy_stats.mode(np.array(obj['bp']['ev']['bitStart']).flatten(), keepdims=False).mode\n   344→    bc_bitstart = np.array(obj['sglx']['bitcode']['bitstart']).flatten()\n   345→    bc_mode = scipy_stats.mode(bc_bitstart, keepdims=False).mode\n   346→    fs = float(obj['sglx']['fs'])\n   347→    vidshift = bc_mode / fs - bitStart\n   348→    return vidshift\n   349→\n   350→\n   351→def get_valid_cluster_indices(clu_probe, excluded_qualities=EXCLUDED_QUALITIES):\n   352→    \"\"\"Find valid cluster indices matching findClusters.m with quality='all'.\"\"\"\n   353→    qualities = clu_probe['quality']\n   354→    valid = []\n   355→    for i, q in enumerate(qualities):\n   356→        if isinstance(q, str):\n   357→            q_stripped = q.strip()\n   358→        else:\n   359→            q_stripped = ''\n   360→        if q_stripped.lower() not in {e.lower() for e in excluded_qualities}:\n   361→            valid.append(i)\n   362→    return valid\n   363→\n   364→\n   365→def align_and_bin_spikes(clu_probe, cluster_indices, align_times, ntrials,\n   366→                          tmin=TMIN, tmax=TMAX, dt=DT, smooth_win=SMOOTH_WINDOW,\n   367→                          bctype=BC_TYPE):\n   368→    \"\"\"Align spikes to event and bin into firing rates.\n   369→\n   370→    Returns:\n   371→        trialdat: (n_time, n_neurons, n_trials) firing rate array\n   372→        time_axis: (n_time,) time axis\n   373→    \"\"\"\n   374→    edges = np.arange(tmin, tmax + dt, dt)\n   375→    # Time axis: center of each bin (matching getSeq.m: edges + dt/2, exclude last)\n   376→    time_axis = edges[:-1] + dt / 2\n   377→    n_time = len(time_axis)\n   378→    n_neurons = len(cluster_indices)\n   379→\n   380→    trialdat = np.zeros((n_time, n_neurons, ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n   381→\n   382→    for i, clu_idx in enumerate(cluster_indices):\n   383→        trial_arr = np.array(clu_probe['trial'][clu_idx]).flatten()\n   384→        trialtm_arr = np.array(clu_probe['trialtm'][clu_idx]).flatten()\n   385→\n   386→        for j in range(ntrials):\n   387→            trial_num = j + 1  # MATLAB 1-indexed\n   388→            spk_mask = trial_arr == trial_num\n   389→            if not np.any(spk_mask):\n   390→                continue\n   391→\n   392→            # Align: trialtm_aligned = trialtm - alignTime\n   393→            aligned = trialtm_arr[spk_mask] - align_times[j]\n   394→\n   395→            # Bin\n   396→            counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned, bins=edges)\n   397→\n   398→            # Convert to firing rate and smooth\n   399→            fr = counts.astype(np.float32) / dt\n   400→            fr_smooth = causal_gaussian_smooth(fr, smooth_win, bctype)\n   401→            trialdat[:, i, j] = fr_smooth\n   402→\n   403→    return trialdat, time_axis\n   404→\n   405→\n   406→def remove_low_fr_clusters(trialdat, cluster_indices, low_fr=LOW_FR):\n   407→    \"\"\"Remove clusters with mean FR < low_fr across all trials.\n   408→    Matches removeLowFRClusters.m: mean of mean across conditions.\"\"\"\n   409→    # Mean FR across all time and trials\n   410→    mean_frs = np.nanmean(np.nanmean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=0)  # (n_neurons,)\n   411→    keep = mean_frs > low_fr\n   412→    return trialdat[:, keep, :], [cluster_indices[i] for i in range(len(cluster_indices)) if keep[i]], keep\n   413→\n   414→\n   415→def get_motion_energy_aligned(me_raw, obj, align_times, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials):\n   416→    \"\"\"Align motion energy to neural time axis matching loadMotionEnergy.m.\"\"\"\n   417→    me_aligned = np.zeros((len(time_axis), ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n   418→\n   419→    traj_view0 = obj['traj'][0]  # side cam\n   420→\n   421→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   422→        me_trial = np.array(me_raw[trix, 0]).flatten()\n   423→\n   424→        try:\n   425→            ft = np.array(traj_view0['frameTimes'][trix]).flatten()\n   426→            old_time = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   427→        except:\n   428→            # Fallback: generate frame times at 400 Hz\n   429→            nframes = len(me_trial)\n   430→            ft = np.arange(1, nframes + 1) / VIDEO_FR\n   431→            old_time = ft - 0.5 - align_times[trix]\n   432→\n   433→        # Interpolate to neural time axis\n   434→        if len(old_time) > 1 and len(me_trial) > 1:\n   435→            f_interp = interp1d(old_time, me_trial, kind='linear',\n   436→                               bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n   437→            me_aligned[:, trix] = f_interp(time_axis)\n   438→\n   439→    # Fill NaN with nearest (matching fillmissing(,'nearest'))\n   440→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   441→        col = me_aligned[:, trix]\n   442→        nans = np.isnan(col)\n   443→        if nans.any() and not nans.all():\n   444→            valid_idx = np.where(~nans)[0]\n   445→            nan_idx = np.where(nans)[0]\n   446→            nearest = np.searchsorted(valid_idx, nan_idx).clip(0, len(valid_idx)-1)\n   447→            col[nans] = col[valid_idx[nearest]]\n   448→            me_aligned[:, trix] = col\n   449→\n   450→    return me_aligned\n   451→\n   452→\n   453→def compute_tongue_velocity(obj, align_times, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials):\n   454→    \"\"\"Compute tongue velocity from DLC bottom camera data.\n   455→    Per paper methods: tongue missing values are NOT filled with nearest.\n   456→    Velocity is 0 where tongue is not visible.\"\"\"\n   457→    traj_bottom = obj['traj'][1]  # bottom cam\n   458→    feat_names_raw = traj_bottom['featNames'][0]\n   459→    feat_names = []\n   460→    for fn in feat_names_raw:\n   461→        if isinstance(fn, list):\n   462→            feat_names.append(fn[0] if fn else '')\n   463→        else:\n   464→            feat_names.append(str(fn))\n   465→\n   466→    # Find tongue feature index (top_tongue)\n   467→    tongue_idx = None\n   468→    for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n   469→        if name == 'top_tongue':\n   470→            tongue_idx = i\n   471→            break\n   472→    if tongue_idx is None:\n   473→        for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n   474→            if 'tongue' in name.lower():\n   475→                tongue_idx = i\n   476→                break\n   477→\n   478→    if tongue_idx is None:\n   479→        return np.full((len(time_axis), ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   480→\n   481→    tongue_vel = np.full((len(time_axis), ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   482→\n   483→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   484→        ts = np.array(traj_bottom['ts'][trix])\n   485→        if ts.ndim < 3:\n   486→            continue\n   487→\n   488→        x = ts[:, 0, tongue_idx].copy()\n   489→        y = ts[:, 1, tongue_idx].copy()\n   490→\n   491→        # DO NOT fill NaN for tongue (per paper methods)\n   492→        # Compute velocity only where tongue is visible (not NaN)\n   493→        vx = np.full_like(x, np.nan)\n   494→        vy = np.full_like(y, np.nan)\n   495→\n   496→        valid = ~np.isnan(x) & ~np.isnan(y)\n   497→        if np.sum(valid) >= 2:\n   498→            # Compute gradient only on valid segments\n   499→            vx_all = np.gradient(x) * VIDEO_FR\n   500→            vy_all = np.gradient(y) * VIDEO_FR\n   501→            speed = np.sqrt(vx_all**2 + vy_all**2)\n   502→            # Only keep speed where tongue is visible\n   503→            speed[~valid] = np.nan\n   504→        else:\n   505→            speed = np.full_like(x, np.nan)\n   506→\n   507→        # Align to neural time axis\n   508→        try:\n   509→            ft = np.array(traj_bottom['frameTimes'][trix]).flatten()\n   510→            old_time = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   511→        except:\n   512→            nframes = ts.shape[0]\n   513→            ft = np.arange(1, nframes + 1) / VIDEO_FR\n   514→            old_time = ft - 0.5 - align_times[trix]\n   515→\n   516→        if len(old_time) > 1:\n   517→            f_interp = interp1d(old_time, speed, kind='linear',\n   518→                               bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n   519→            tongue_vel[:, trix] = f_interp(time_axis)\n   520→\n   521→    # Set NaN to 0 (tongue not visible = no tongue movement)\n   522→    tongue_vel = np.nan_to_num(tongue_vel, nan=0.0)\n   523→\n   524→    return tongue_vel\n   525→\n   526→\n   527→def compute_paw_velocity(obj, align_times, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials):\n   528→    \"\"\"Compute paw velocity from DLC bottom camera data.\"\"\"\n   529→    traj_bottom = obj['traj'][1]  # bottom cam\n   530→    feat_names_raw = traj_bottom['featNames'][0]\n   531→    feat_names = []\n   532→    for fn in feat_names_raw:\n   533→        if isinstance(fn, list):\n   534→            feat_names.append(fn[0] if fn else '')\n   535→        else:\n   536→            feat_names.append(str(fn))\n   537→\n   538→    # Find paw indices (top_paw, bottom_paw)\n   539→    paw_indices = []\n   540→    for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n   541→        if 'paw' in name.lower():\n   542→            paw_indices.append(i)\n   543→\n   544→    if not paw_indices:\n   545→        return np.zeros((len(time_axis), ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n   546→\n   547→    paw_vel = np.zeros((len(time_axis), ntrials), dtype=np.float32)\n   548→\n   549→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   550→        ts = np.array(traj_bottom['ts'][trix])\n   551→        if ts.ndim < 3:\n   552→            continue\n   553→\n   554→        speeds = []\n   555→        for pidx in paw_indices:\n   556→            x = ts[:, 0, pidx].copy()\n   557→            y = ts[:, 1, pidx].copy()\n   558→\n   559→            for arr in [x, y]:\n   560→                nans = np.isnan(arr)\n   561→                if nans.any() and not nans.all():\n   562→                    valid = np.where(~nans)[0]\n   563→                    nan_pos = np.where(nans)[0]\n   564→                    nearest = np.searchsorted(valid, nan_pos).clip(0, len(valid)-1)\n   565→                    arr[nans] = arr[valid[nearest]]\n   566→\n   567→            vx = np.gradient(x) * VIDEO_FR\n   568→            vy = np.gradient(y) * VIDEO_FR\n   569→            speeds.append(np.sqrt(vx**2 + vy**2))\n   570→\n   571→        # Average across paw features\n   572→        avg_speed = np.mean(speeds, axis=0)\n   573→\n   574→        try:\n   575→            ft = np.array(traj_bottom['frameTimes'][trix]).flatten()\n   576→            old_time = ft - vidshift - align_times[trix]\n   577→        except:\n   578→            nframes = ts.shape[0]\n   579→            ft = np.arange(1, nframes + 1) / VIDEO_FR\n   580→            old_time = ft - 0.5 - align_times[trix]\n   581→\n   582→        if len(old_time) > 1:\n   583→            f_interp = interp1d(old_time, avg_speed, kind='linear',\n   584→                               bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n   585→            paw_vel[:, trix] = f_interp(time_axis)\n   586→\n   587→    # Fill NaN\n   588→    for trix in range(ntrials):\n   589→        col = paw_vel[:, trix]\n   590→        nans = np.isnan(col)\n   591→        if nans.any() and not nans.all():\n   592→            valid_idx = np.where(~nans)[0]\n   593→            nan_idx = np.where(nans)[0]\n   594→            nearest = np.searchsorted(valid_idx, nan_idx).clip(0, len(valid_idx)-1)\n   595→            col[nans] = col[valid_idx[nearest]]\n   596→\n   597→    return paw_vel\n   598→\n   599→\n   600→def discretize_per_session(data_2d, percentile=50):\n   601→    \"\"\"Discretize time-varying data using per-session threshold.\n   602→    data_2d: (n_time, n_trials)\n   603→    Returns: (n_time, n_trials) binary array (0: < threshold, 1: >= threshold)\n   604→    \"\"\"\n   605→    all_vals = data_2d[~np.isnan(data_2d)]\n   606→    if len(all_vals) == 0:\n   607→        return np.zeros_like(data_2d, dtype=np.int32)\n   608→    threshold = np.percentile(all_vals, percentile)\n   609→    # If threshold is 0 (e.g. speed data with many zeros), use a tiny positive value\n   610→    # so that exact zeros become \"low\" and any positive value is \"high\"\n   611→    if threshold == 0:\n   612→        threshold = np.finfo(np.float32).eps\n   613→    result = (data_2d >= threshold).astype(np.int32)\n   614→    # Replace any remaining NaN positions with 0\n   615→    result[np.isnan(data_2d)] = 0\n   616→    return result\n   617→\n   618→\n   619→# ============================================================\n   620→# MAIN PROCESSING\n   621→# ============================================================\n   622→\n   623→def process_session(anm, date, probes, data_dir, show_processing=False, session_idx=0):\n   624→    \"\"\"Process a single session and return formatted data.\"\"\"\n   625→\n   626→    t_start = time.time()\n   627→\n   628→    # Load data\n   629→    obj, me_raw, me_thresh, t_load = load_session_data(anm, date, data_dir)\n   630→    print(f\"  Loaded in {t_load:.1f}s\")\n   631→\n   632→    bp = obj['bp']\n   633→    ntrials_total = int(bp['Ntrials'])\n   634→\n   635→    # Get event times\n   636→    ev = bp['ev']\n   637→    goCue = np.array(ev['goCue']).flatten()\n   638→\n   639→    # Get trial masks\n   640→    R = np.array(bp['R']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   641→    L = np.array(bp['L']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   642→    hit = np.array(bp['hit']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   643→    miss = np.array(bp['miss']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   644→    no = np.array(bp['no']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   645→    autowater = np.array(bp['autowater']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   646→    early = np.array(bp['early']).flatten().astype(bool)\n   647→\n   648→    stim_enable = np.zeros(ntrials_total, dtype=bool)\n   649→    stim = bp.get('stim')\n   650→    if stim is not None and isinstance(stim, dict):\n   651→        se = stim.get('enable')\n   652→        if se is not None:\n   653→            stim_enable = np.array(se).flatten().astype(bool)\n   654→\n   655→    # Trial selection: exclude early, stim, and no-response (ignore) trials\n   656→    valid_mask = ~early & ~stim_enable & ~no\n   657→    # Also need a lick response (hit or miss)\n   658→    valid_mask = valid_mask & (hit | miss)\n   659→\n   660→    valid_trials = np.where(valid_mask)[0]  # 0-indexed\n   661→    n_valid = len(valid_trials)\n   662→\n   663→    if n_valid < 2:\n   664→        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {n_valid} valid trials, skipping session\")\n   665→        return None\n   666→\n   667→    print(f\"  Trials: {ntrials_total} total, {n_valid} valid ({n_valid/ntrials_total*100:.0f}%)\")\n   668→\n   669→    # Compute video offset\n   670→    vidshift = compute_video_offset(obj)\n   671→\n   672→    # Get alignment times\n   673→    align_times_all = goCue  # for all trials\n   674→\n   675→    # Process neural data from specified probes\n   676→    all_cluster_indices = []\n   677→    clu_data = obj['clu']\n   678→\n   679→    for probe_num in probes:\n   680→        probe_idx = probe_num - 1  # 0-indexed\n   681→        clu_probe = clu_data[probe_idx]\n   682→\n   683→        if clu_probe is None:\n   684→            continue\n   685→\n   686→        if isinstance(clu_probe, dict):\n   687→            valid_clu = get_valid_cluster_indices(clu_probe)\n   688→            all_cluster_indices.append((probe_idx, valid_clu))\n   689→            print(f\"  Probe {probe_num}: {len(clu_probe['quality'])} total, {len(valid_clu)} valid clusters\")\n   690→\n   691→    # Bin and smooth spikes for all valid clusters across probes\n   692→    t_bin_start = time.time()\n   693→    edges = np.arange(TMIN, TMAX + DT, DT)\n   694→    time_axis = edges[:-1] + DT / 2\n   695→    n_time = len(time_axis)\n   696→\n   697→    # Count total neurons\n   698→    total_neurons = sum(len(v) for _, v in all_cluster_indices)\n   699→\n   700→    # Process spikes for all neurons at once\n   701→    trialdat = np.zeros((n_time, total_neurons, ntrials_total), dtype=np.float32)\n   702→\n   703→    neuron_offset = 0\n   704→    for probe_idx, valid_clu in all_cluster_indices:\n   705→        clu_probe = clu_data[probe_idx]\n   706→\n   707→        for i, clu_idx in enumerate(valid_clu):\n   708→            trial_arr = np.array(clu_probe['trial'][clu_idx]).flatten()\n   709→            trialtm_arr = np.array(clu_probe['trialtm'][clu_idx]).flatten()\n   710→\n   711→            for j in range(ntrials_total):\n   712→                trial_num = j + 1\n   713→                spk_mask = trial_arr == trial_num\n   714→                if not np.any(spk_mask):\n   715→                    continue\n   716→\n   717→                aligned = trialtm_arr[spk_mask] - align_times_all[j]\n   718→                counts, _ = np.histogram(aligned, bins=edges)\n   719→                fr = counts.astype(np.float32) / DT\n   720→                trialdat[:, neuron_offset + i, j] = causal_gaussian_smooth(fr, SMOOTH_WINDOW, BC_TYPE)\n   721→\n   722→        neuron_offset += len(valid_clu)\n   723→\n   724→    t_bin = time.time() - t_bin_start\n   725→    print(f\"  Spike binning: {t_bin:.1f}s for {total_neurons} neurons\")\n   726→\n   727→    # Remove low FR clusters\n   728→    trialdat, kept_mask = remove_low_fr_neurons(trialdat, LOW_FR)\n   729→    n_neurons_final = trialdat.shape[1]\n   730→    print(f\"  After FR filter (>{LOW_FR} Hz): {n_neurons_final} neurons (removed {total_neurons - n_neurons_final})\")\n   731→\n   732→    if n_neurons_final < 10:\n   733→        print(f\"  WARNING: Only {n_neurons_final} neurons after filtering, skipping session\")\n   734→        return None\n   735→\n   736→    # Extract valid trials only\n   737→    trialdat_valid = trialdat[:, :, valid_trials]  # (n_time, n_neurons, n_valid)\n   738→\n   739→    # Process behavioral outputs\n   740→    # 1. Lick direction: R&hit or L&miss -> right(1), L&hit or R&miss -> left(0)\n   741→    lick_right = (R & hit) | (L & miss)\n   742→    lick_direction = lick_right[valid_trials].astype(np.int32)\n   743→\n   744→    # 2. Context: autowater -> WC(0), ~autowater -> DR(1)\n   745→    context = (~autowater[valid_trials]).astype(np.int32)\n   746→\n   747→    # 3. Outcome: hit -> correct(1), miss -> incorrect(0)\n   748→    outcome = hit[valid_trials].astype(np.int32)\n   749→\n   750→    # Process behavioral time series\n   751→    t_behav_start = time.time()\n   752→\n   753→    # 4. Tongue velocity\n   754→    tongue_vel = compute_tongue_velocity(obj, align_times_all, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials_total)\n   755→    tongue_vel_valid = tongue_vel[:, valid_trials]\n   756→    tongue_vel_disc = discretize_per_session(tongue_vel_valid)\n   757→\n   758→    # 5. Paw velocity\n   759→    paw_vel = compute_paw_velocity(obj, align_times_all, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials_total)\n   760→    paw_vel_valid = paw_vel[:, valid_trials]\n   761→    paw_vel_disc = discretize_per_session(paw_vel_valid)\n   762→\n   763→    # 6. Motion energy\n   764→    if me_raw is not None:\n   765→        me_aligned = get_motion_energy_aligned(me_raw, obj, align_times_all, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials_total)\n   766→        me_valid = me_aligned[:, valid_trials]\n   767→        me_disc = discretize_per_session(me_valid)\n   768→    else:\n   769→        me_disc = np.zeros((n_time, n_valid), dtype=np.int32)\n   770→\n   771→    t_behav = time.time() - t_behav_start\n   772→    print(f\"  Behavioral processing: {t_behav:.1f}s\")\n   773→\n   774→    # Build trial-level data structures\n   775→    neural_trials = []\n   776→    input_trials = []\n   777→    output_trials = []\n   778→\n   779→    for t_idx in range(n_valid):\n   780→        # Neural: (n_neurons, n_time)\n   781→        neural_trials.append(trialdat_valid[:, :, t_idx].T.astype(np.float32))\n   782→\n   783→        # Input: time from goCue in seconds (1, n_time)\n   784→        input_trials.append(time_axis.reshape(1, -1).astype(np.float32))\n   785→\n   786→        # Output: (n_output, n_time) for time-varying, (n_output,) for per-trial\n   787→        # Outputs 0-2 are per-trial, 3-5 are time-varying\n   788→        out = np.zeros((6, n_time), dtype=np.int32)\n   789→        out[0, :] = lick_direction[t_idx]  # broadcast per-trial to time\n   790→        out[1, :] = context[t_idx]\n   791→        out[2, :] = outcome[t_idx]\n   792→        out[3, :] = tongue_vel_disc[:, t_idx]\n   793→        out[4, :] = paw_vel_disc[:, t_idx]\n   794→        out[5, :] = me_disc[:, t_idx]\n   795→        output_trials.append(out)\n   796→\n   797→    # Show processing plots\n   798→    if show_processing and n_valid > 0:\n   799→        plot_processing(anm, date, session_idx, time_axis, trialdat_valid,\n   800→                       tongue_vel_valid, paw_vel_valid,\n   801→                       me_valid if me_raw is not None else None,\n   802→                       tongue_vel_disc, paw_vel_disc, me_disc,\n   803→                       lick_direction, context, outcome, valid_trials, align_times_all)\n   804→\n   805→    t_total = time.time() - t_start\n   806→    print(f\"  Total: {t_total:.1f}s, {n_valid} trials, {n_neurons_final} neurons\")\n   807→\n   808→    return {\n   809→        'neural': neural_trials,\n   810→        'input': input_trials,\n   811→        'output': output_trials,\n   812→        'animal': anm,\n   813→        'n_neurons': n_neurons_final,\n   814→        'n_trials': n_valid,\n   815→    }\n   816→\n   817→\n   818→def remove_low_fr_neurons(trialdat, low_fr):\n   819→    \"\"\"Remove neurons with mean FR below threshold.\n   820→    trialdat: (n_time, n_neurons, n_trials)\n   821→    \"\"\"\n   822→    mean_fr = np.nanmean(np.nanmean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=0)\n   823→    keep = mean_fr > low_fr\n   824→    return trialdat[:, keep, :], keep\n   825→\n   826→\n   827→def plot_processing(anm, date, session_idx, time_axis, trialdat,\n   828→                   tongue_vel, paw_vel, me_aligned,\n   829→                   tongue_disc, paw_disc, me_disc,\n   830→                   lick_dir, context, outcome, valid_trials, align_times):\n   831→    \"\"\"Plot processing steps for visual inspection.\"\"\"\n   832→    fig, axes = plt.subplots(4, 3, figsize=(18, 16))\n   833→    fig.suptitle(f'Processing: {anm}_{date}', fontsize=14)\n   834→\n   835→    n_trials_plot = min(5, trialdat.shape[2])\n   836→\n   837→    # Row 0: Neural activity (few neurons, few trials)\n   838→    ax = axes[0, 0]\n   839→    n_neurons_plot = min(5, trialdat.shape[1])\n   840→    for n in range(n_neurons_plot):\n   841→        ax.plot(time_axis, trialdat[:, n, 0], alpha=0.7, label=f'N{n}')\n   842→    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)\n   843→    ax.set_title('Neural (trial 0)')\n   844→    ax.set_ylabel('FR (spk/s)')\n   845→    ax.legend(fontsize=6)\n   846→\n   847→    ax = axes[0, 1]\n   848→    # Trial-averaged for one neuron\n   849→    ax.plot(time_axis, np.mean(trialdat[:, 0, :], axis=1), 'k-')\n   850→    ax.axvline(0, color='r', linestyle='--')\n   851→    ax.set_title(f'Neuron 0 mean (n={trialdat.shape[2]} trials)')\n   852→\n   858→    ax = axes[1, 0]\n   859→    for t in range(n_trials_plot):\n   860→        ax.plot(time_axis, tongue_vel[:, t], alpha=0.5)\n   861→    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--')\n   862→    ax.set_title('Tongue velocity (raw)')\n   863→\n   864→    ax = axes[1, 1]\n   865→    for t in range(n_trials_plot):\n   866→        ax.plot(time_axis, tongue_disc[:, t], alpha=0.5)\n   867→    ax.set_title('Tongue velocity (discretized)')\n   868→\n   869→    ax = axes[1, 2]\n   870→    ax.bar(['DR', 'WC'], [np.sum(context == 1), np.sum(context == 0)])\n   871→    ax.set_title('Context')\n   872→\n   873→    # Row 2: Paw velocity\n   874→    ax = axes[2, 0]\n   875→    for t in range(n_trials_plot):\n   876→        ax.plot(time_axis, paw_vel[:, t], alpha=0.5)\n   877→    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--')\n   878→    ax.set_title('Paw velocity (raw)')\n   879→\n   880→    ax = axes[2, 1]\n   881→    for t in range(n_trials_plot):\n   882→        ax.plot(time_axis, paw_disc[:, t], alpha=0.5)\n   883→    ax.set_title('Paw velocity (discretized)')\n   884→\n   885→    ax = axes[2, 2]\n   886→    ax.bar(['Incorrect', 'Correct'], [np.sum(outcome == 0), np.sum(outcome == 1)])\n   887→    ax.set_title('Outcome')\n   888→\n   889→    # Row 3: Motion energy\n   890→    ax = axes[3, 0]\n   891→    if me_aligned is not None:\n   892→        for t in range(n_trials_plot):\n   893→            ax.plot(time_axis, me_aligned[:, t], alpha=0.5)\n   894→    ax.axvline(0, color='k', linestyle='--')\n   895→    ax.set_title('Motion energy (raw)')\n   896→\n   897→    ax = axes[3, 1]\n   898→    for t in range(n_trials_plot):\n   899→        ax.plot(time_axis, me_disc[:, t], alpha=0.5)\n   900→    ax.set_title('Motion energy (discretized)')\n   901→\n   902→    ax = axes[3, 2]\n   903→    ax.text(0.5, 0.5, f'N neurons: {trialdat.shape[1]}\\nN trials: {trialdat.shape[2]}',\n   904→            transform=ax.transAxes, ha='center', va='center', fontsize=12)\n   905→    ax.set_title('Summary')\n   906→\n   907→    for ax_row in axes:\n   908→        for ax in ax_row:\n   909→            ax.set_xlabel('Time from goCue (s)')\n   910→\n   911→    fig.tight_layout()\n   912→    fig.savefig(f'processing_{anm}_{date}.png', dpi=100)\n   913→    plt.close(fig)\n   914→    print(f\"  Saved processing plot: processing_{anm}_{date}.png\")\n   915→\n   916→\n   917→def main():\n   918→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n   919→    parser.add_argument('output', type=str, help='Output pickle file')\n   920→    parser.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true', help='Process only 2 sessions')\n   921→    parser.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', help='Process all sessions (default)')\n   922→    parser.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true', help='Save processing plots')\n   923→    args = parser.parse_args()\n   924→\n   925→    if args.sample:\n   926→        sessions = ALL_SESSIONS[:2]\n   927→        print(f\"SAMPLE MODE: Processing {len(sessions)} sessions\")\n   928→    else:\n   929→        sessions = ALL_SESSIONS\n   930→        print(f\"FULL MODE: Processing {len(sessions)} sessions\")\n   931→\n   932→    show_processing = args.show_processing\n   933→    if show_processing and not args.sample:\n   934→        # Only show processing for first 2 sessions in full mode\n   935→        show_limit = 2\n   936→    else:\n   937→        show_limit = len(sessions)\n   938→\n   939→    # Collect all session data\n   940→    all_neural = []\n   941→    all_input = []\n   942→    all_output = []\n   943→    all_animals = []\n   944→    all_brain_region_idx = []\n   945→    session_info = []\n   946→\n   947→    subjects_set = set()\n   948→\n   949→    t_total_start = time.time()\n   950→\n   951→    for i, (anm, date, probes, data_dir) in enumerate(sessions):\n   952→        print(f\"\\n[{i+1}/{len(sessions)}] Processing {anm}_{date} (probes {probes})...\")\n   953→\n   954→        result = process_session(anm, date, probes, data_dir,\n   955→                                show_processing=(show_processing and i < show_limit),\n   956→                                session_idx=i)\n   957→\n   958→        if result is None:\n   959→            continue\n   960→\n   961→        all_neural.append(result['neural'])\n   962→        all_input.append(result['input'])\n   963→        all_output.append(result['output'])\n   964→        all_animals.append(anm)\n   965→        subjects_set.add(anm)\n   966→\n   967→        # Brain region: all neurons are ALM\n   968→        all_brain_region_idx.append(np.zeros(result['n_neurons'], dtype=np.int64))\n   969→\n   970→        session_info.append({\n   971→            'animal': anm,\n   972→            'date': date,\n   973→            'data_dir': data_dir,\n   974→            'probes': probes,\n   975→            'n_neurons': result['n_neurons'],\n   976→            'n_trials': result['n_trials'],\n   977→        })\n   978→\n   979→    t_total = time.time() - t_total_start\n   980→    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n   981→    print(f\"Processing complete: {len(all_neural)} sessions in {t_total:.1f}s\")\n   982→\n   983→    # Build subjects list\n   984→    subjects = sorted(subjects_set)\n   985→    subject_idx = np.array([subjects.index(anm) for anm in all_animals], dtype=np.int64)\n   986→\n   987→    # Build final data dict\n   988→    data = {\n   989→        'neural': all_neural,\n   990→        'input': all_input,\n   991→        'output': all_output,\n   992→\n   993→        'subjects': subjects,\n   994→        'subject_idx': subject_idx,\n   995→\n   996→        'brain_regions': ['ALM'],\n   997→        'brain_region_idx': all_brain_region_idx,\n   998→\n   999→        'input_names': ['time_from_gocue'],\n  1000→        'output_names': ['lick_direction', 'context', 'outcome', 'tongue_velocity', 'paw_velocity', 'motion_energy'],\n  1001→        'output_values': [\n  1002→            ['left', 'right'],           # lick_direction: 0=left, 1=right\n  1003→            ['WC', 'DR'],                # context: 0=WC, 1=DR\n  1004→            ['incorrect', 'correct'],    # outcome: 0=incorrect, 1=correct\n  1005→            ['low', 'high'],             # tongue_velocity: 0=<50th, 1=>=50th\n  1006→            ['low', 'high'],             # paw_velocity: 0=<50th, 1=>=50th\n  1007→            ['low', 'high'],             # motion_energy: 0=<50th, 1=>=50th\n  1008→        ],\n  1009→\n  1010→        'metadata': {\n  1011→            'task_description': 'Two-context delayed-response (DR) and water-cued (WC) directional licking task with ALM recordings',\n  1012→            'time_bin_size': DT * 1000,  # 10 ms\n  1013→            'temporal_alignment_event': 'Go cue onset',\n  1014→            'off_start': TMIN,  # -2.5 s\n  1015→            'off_end': TMAX,    # 2.5 s\n  1016→            'align_event': ALIGN_EVENT,\n  1017→            'smoothing': f'Causal Gaussian, window={SMOOTH_WINDOW} bins',\n  1018→            'fr_threshold': LOW_FR,\n  1019→            'session_info': session_info,\n  1020→            'n_sessions': len(all_neural),\n  1021→            'total_neurons': sum(s['n_neurons'] for s in session_info),\n  1022→            'total_trials': sum(s['n_trials'] for s in session_info),\n  1023→        }\n  1024→    }\n  1025→\n  1026→    # Print summary\n  1027→    print(f\"\\nDataset Summary:\")\n  1028→    print(f\"  Sessions: {len(all_neural)}\")\n  1029→    print(f\"  Subjects: {len(subjects)} ({', '.join(subjects)})\")\n  1030→    print(f\"  Total neurons: {data['metadata']['total_neurons']}\")\n  1031→    print(f\"  Total trials: ============================================================\n\ndef causal_gaussian_smooth(x, N, bctype='reflect'):\n    \"\"\"Causal Gaussian smoothing matching mySmooth.m.\n    x: (T,) or (T, C) array\n    N: window size in bins\n    \"\"\"\n    if N <= 1:\n        return x\n\n    was_1d = x.ndim == 1\n    if was_1d:\n        x = x[:, None]\n\n    # Build causal Gaussian kernel\n    kern = align_and_bin_spikes(clu_probe, cluster_indices, align_times, ntrials,\n                          tmin=TMIN, tmax=TMAX, dt=DT, smooth_win=SMOOTH_WINDOW,\n                          bctype=BC_TYPE):\n    \"\"\"Align spikes to event and bin into firing rates.\n\n    Returns:\n        trialdat: (n_time, n_neurons, n_trials) firing rate array\n        time_axis: (n_time,) time axis\n    \"\"\"\n remove_low_fr_clusters(trialdat, cluster_indices, low_fr=LOW_FR):\n    \"\"\"Remove clusters with mean FR < low_fr across all trials.\n    Matches removeLowFRClusters.m: mean of mean across conditions.\"\"\"\n    # Mean FR across all time and trials\n    mean_frs = np.nanmean(np.nanmean(trialdat, axis=2), axis=0)  # (n_neurons,)\n    keep = mean_frs > low_fr\n    return trialdat[:, keep, align_times[trix]\n        except:\n            # Fallback: generate frame times at 400 Hz\n            nframes = len(me_trial)\n            ft = np.arange(1, nframes + 1) / VIDEO_FR\n       me_aligned[:, trix] = col\n\n    return me_aligned\n\n\ndef compute_tongue_velocity(obj, align_times, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Compute tongue velocity from DLC bottom camera data.\n    Per paper methods: tongue missing values are NOT filled with nearest.\n    Velocity is 0 where tongue is not visible.\"\"\"\n    traj_bottom = obj['traj'][1]  # bottom cam\n    feat_names_raw = traj_bottom['featNames'][0]\n    feat_names = []\n    for fn in feat_names_raw:\n        if isinstance(fn, list):\n            feat_names.append(fn[0] if fn else '')\n        else:\n            feat_names.append(str(fn))\n\n    # Find tongue feature index (top_tongue)\n    tongue_idx = None\n    for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n        if name == 'top_tongue':\n            tongue_idx = i\n            break\n    if tongue_idx is None:\n        for i, name in enumerate(feat_names):\n            if 'tongue' in name.lower():\n                tongue_idx = i\n                break\n\n    if tongue_idx is None:\n        return np.full((len(time_axis), ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    tongue_vel = np.full((len(time_axis), ntrials), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n\n    for trix in range(ntrials):\n        ts = np.array(traj_bottom['ts'][trix])\n        if ts.ndim < 3:\n            continue\n\n        x = ts[:, 0, tongue_idx].copy()\n        y = ts[:, 1, tongue_idx].copy()\n\n        # DO NOT fill NaN for tongue (per paper methods)\n        # Compute velocity only where tongue is visible (not NaN)\n        vx = np.full_like(x, np.nan)\n        vy = np.full_like(y, np.nan)\n\n        valid = ~np.isnan(x) & ~np.isnan(y)\n        if np.sum(valid) >= 2:\n            # Compute gradient only on valid segments\n            vx_all = np.gradient(x) * VIDEO_FR\n            vy_all = np.gradient(y) * VIDEO_FR\n            speed = np.sqrt(vx_all**2 + vy_all**2)\n            # Only keep speed where tongue is visible\n            speed[~valid] = np.nan\n        else:\n            speed = np.full_like(x, np.nan)\n\n        # Align to neural time axis\n        try:\n            ft = np.array(traj_bottom['frameTimes'][trix]).flatten()\n align_times[trix]\n\n        if len(old_time) > 1:\n            f_interp = interp1d(old_time, speed, kind='linear',\n                               bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan)\n            tongue_vel[:, trix] = f_interp(time_axis)\n\n    # Set NaN to 0 (tongue not visible = no tongue movement)\n    tongue_vel = np.nan_to_num(tongue_vel, nan=0.0)\n\n    return tongue_vel\n\n\ndef compute_paw_velocity(obj, align_times, time_axis, vidshift, ntrials):\n    \"\"\"Compute paw velocity from DLC bottom camera data.\"\"\"\n    traj_bottom = obj['traj'][1]  # bottom cam\n    feat_names_raw = traj_bottom['featNames'][0]\n    feat_names = []\n    for fn in feat_names_raw:\n        if isinstance(fn, list):\n            feat_names.append(fn[0] if fn else '')\n        else:\n
